settings-only.json
0 transactions ready to import
Merge adds new transactions and keeps everything you have. Budgets are combined (imported categories win), new goals and templates are added, and your currency and Safe to Spend settings stay as they are.
Replace swaps your transactions for the file's. Budgets, goals, templates, currency and Safe to Spend are replaced only if the file has a valid section for them. A recovery copy of your current data is kept first, and you can undo straight after.
Merge — add new, keep existingReplace — use the file's dataCancel
